## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else, and I'll tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 할 수 있는 일을 맡아 드릴게요. 예를 들어 "이 프로젝트에서 실패하는 테스트 고쳐 줘"처럼 말씀하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 오늘 밤 푹 쉬세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] **TCP は信頼性を重視して届くことを保証し、UDP は保証を捨てて速さと軽さを優先します。**
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de compartirla: poner tus commits encima de la última `main`, o limpiarlos con squash, para un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la usan otros, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe el trabajo de quien ya los tiene.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、可靠有序；UDP 无连接、只管尽力发送，不保证到达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own private branch to bring it up to date and keep history clean, before anyone else has pulled it. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits that others may have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，bun 会按路径里的字符串匹配：

```
bun test foo
```

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```
bun test -t "登录成功"
```

这三种可以组合，比如 `bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "登录成功"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where the database writes every change before applying it to the main data, so a crash mid-write can be recovered by replaying the log. It trades one extra sequential write per change for durability and fast restarts without losing committed data.
```

```
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="big">–</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">No runs yet</div>

function render(runs) {

  var big = document.getElementById('big');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  runs.forEach(function (r) {

    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';

    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = r.writes + ' w/s';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(s);

    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
```

```
  var cur = "2026-11-20";

  function $(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
  function el(tag, cls, text){ var e = document.createElement(tag); if(cls) e.className = cls; if(text!=null) e.textContent = text; return e; }
  function pad(n){ return n < 10 ? "0" + n : "" + n; }
  function parse(iso){ var p = iso.split("-"); return new Date(+p[0], +p[1]-1, +p[2]); }
  function label(iso){ var d = parse(iso); return (d.getMonth()+1) + "/" + d.getD … );
      });

    });

  function renderChips(){
    var p = $("pick"); p.innerHTML = "";
    S.days.forEach(function(day){
      var b = el("button", "k-chip", label(day));
      b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", day === cur ? "true" : "false");
      b.addEventListener("click", function(){ cur = day; renderChips(); });
      p.appendChild(b);
    });
  }
  $("nextday").addEventListener("click", function(){
    var t = parse(S.days[S.days.length-1]);
    t.setDate(t.getDate() + 1);
    var iso = t.getFullYear() + "-" + pad(t.getMonth()+1) + "-" + pad(t.getDate());
    S.days.push(iso); cur = iso; save();
  });
  $("add").addEventListener("click", function(){
    var a = parseFloat($("amt").value);
    if (!(a > 0)) return;
    var name = $("name").value.trim() || "花费";
    S.items.push({ day: cur, name: name, amount: Math.round(a * 100) / 100 });
    $("name").value = ""; $("amt").value = "";
    $("add").parentNode.removeAttribute("open");
    save();
  });
  ducter.onChange(function(){ load(); render(); });
  load(); render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
};
```

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub">tap a PR when it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<style>
.done .k-row-main span{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.tick{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;border:1.5px solid var(--d-line);margin-right:10px;flex:none;color:var(--d-good);font-weight:700}
.done .tick{background:var(--d-good);border-color:var(--d-good);color:var(--d-card)}
.k-row{cursor:pointer}
</style>
<script>
const DEFAULT=[
 {id:"412",title:"wey-gu/ducter#412 · Compaction cut",waiting:"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday",done:false},
 {id:"10287",title:"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · Retry on 502",waiting:"On CI, failing",done:false},
 {id:"1592",title:"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · Flaky compaction test",waiting:"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday",done:false},
 {id:"418",title:"wey-gu/ducter#418 · Apps bar",waiting:"Draft, not yet sent for review",done:false}
];
let items=ducter.get('prs')||DEFAULT;
function render(){
  const list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  items.forEach((it,i)=>{
    const row=document.createElement('div');
    row.className='k-row'+(it.done?' done':'');
    row.setAttribute('role','button');
    row.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!it.done));
    const tick=document.createElement('span');
    tick.className='tick';
    tick.textContent=it.done?'✓':'';
    const main=document.createElement('span');
    main.className='k-row-main';
    const t=document.createElement('span');
    t.textContent=it.title;
    main.appendChild(t);
    const sub=document.createElement('span');
    sub.className='k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent=it.waiting;
    const wrap=document.createElement('span');
    wrap.style.display='flex';
    wrap.style.alignItems='center';
    wrap.appendChild(tick);
    wrap.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(wrap);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.onclick=()=>{
      items[i].done=!items[i].done;
      ducter.set('prs',items);
      render();
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  const open=items.filter(x=>!x.done).length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent=open;
  ducter.summary(open+' waiting');
}
ducter.onChange(()=>{items=ducter.get('prs')||DEFAULT;render();});
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · Compaction cut","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · Retry on 502","waiting":"On CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · Flaky compaction test","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · Apps bar","waiting":"Draft, not yet sent for review","done":false}]}
```

### app-renew-zh

```
(earlier) user: 帮我订明天去北京的高铁，再订两晚酒店，离 agibar 近一点
(earlier) partner: 订好了：11/20 G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南，¥662；亚朵酒店（望京）11/20 和 11/21 两晚，¥1,036。
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 把「出差花销」换成新样子，能填的先帮我填好
  [partner message] 「出差花销」已换成新样子，北京这趟的高铁 ¥662 和亚朵两晚 ¥1,036 已经填进去了，合计 ¥1,698。
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2462 characters): <main class="k-app"><div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">这趟出差合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没有花销</div></div><h2 class="k-h">记一笔</h2><div class="k-card"><div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="比如 北京出差 11/20–21"></div><div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div><div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="餐饮">餐饮</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="其他">其他</button></div></div><div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div><button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button></div><h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2><div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div></main><script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通";
const money=n=>"¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US");
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(x=>x.setAttribute("aria-pressed",x===b))});
function el(tag,cls,text){const e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;e.textContent=text;return e}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  $("total").textContent=money(total);
  $("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没有花销";
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total):"暂无花销");
  const list=$("list");list.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){list.appendChild(el("div","k-empty","还没有花销，在上面记第一笔。"));return}
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const r=el("div","k-row");
    const main=el("div","k-row-main",x.trip||"未命名出差");
    main.appendChild(el("div","k-row-sub",[x.day,x.cat,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")));
    r.appendChild(main);
    r.appendChild(el("div","k-row-value",money(x.amt)));
    list.appendChild(r);
  });
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"未命名出差",amt:amt,day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一台米白色的 3D 小笔记本电脑，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色。整体是软萌的粘土风插画。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好对应“这里还没有内容”，用来表示没有文件、没有记录这类情况很自然。奶白色调也温和，不会让人觉得是报错。

有两点要注意：一是它是 320×246 的位图，显示时别放大太多，建议在 160–200 像素宽左右使用，高清屏可以另外准备 2 倍图；二是深色模式下，米白色主体和浅色背景的对比可能会变弱，最好给它留一块浅色底，或者换一版深色适配的图。
```

```
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 我去给 TTL 测试补一条正好到期那一刻的用例，补好了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

要求：
- 在现有的 TTL 测试里（继续用假时钟）加一条用例：把时间推到恰好等于过期时刻，断言此时值已经过期（或按现有约定的边界语义，明确写清楚等于时算未过期还是已过期，并在测试名里体现）。
- 同时覆盖到期前一刻（还没过期）作为对照，确保边界两侧行为都对。
- 不要改动已有测试的断言，只新增用例。
- 完成标准：整套测试通过；连跑 20 次都通过；报告里给出测试输出和新用例的名字。
- 范围外：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。在原分支上提交即可。
```
